# Chapter 2 AI Approaches in Intelligent Transportation Systems

K. NITHYA,^([¹](#fn1))

## Abstract

Modern society is reacting according to the decision of intelligence supports. Two such influencing technologies are IoT and AI. Most of the lives and the valuable times of humans are saved using the intelligent transportation systems (ITS). This chapter covers various functionalities of IoT-enabled transportation systems using smart devices like sensors and its usage in three verticals such as on road sensors to control the traffic and efficient parking, in vehicle sensors to prevent accidents and on body sensors to monitor the driver’s condition. And then it is broadened towards the AI functionalities in ITS where the IoT network is deployed. The focus on safety, traffic management, parking management, electronic toll collection, automatic fare collection, etc., are bringing up the cities and its transport facilities on the roadside in a better way. Speed alert system, emergency vehicle notification, driver drowsiness detection, etc., are the supportive systems to ensure public safety in all aspects. As an exemplar, a model is implemented and tested using a kind of on body sensor as eye blink sensor to detect driver’s drowsiness in a smart way. Further, with the output of the sensor an AI technique as CNN algorithm is used to classify and decide the feel of the driver accurately. This helps to take the further action to alert the driver by its decision. This implementation ensures safety for the passengers and prevents accidents.

## 2.1 Introduction

Entire world is getting revolutionized with the help of smart technology and its various advancements. The real time scenarios are being reflected in implementations of smart city projects like transportation, healthcare systems, infrastructure, accessibility, etc., among all, the transport Management system plays an important role in developing smart cities. As per the Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport, the government policy experiences more inclusive growth in the construction of bridges and roads to make the transportation in an intelligent way which directly supports the country’s economic growth and globalization. In another perspective it reflects in the employment opportunities involved in the transport sector such as car, taxi, van, and bus drivers, conductors, railroad workers, road construction labors, supervisor, public transportation inspector, streetcar operator, logistician, traffic supervisor, transportation manager, transportation planner, travel coordinator, tourism, etc. In the developing nation, mobility is required in high aspects for the people to travel for daily work, shopping, and for many social reasons. In general, transport is of two categories: private and public. To travel from one location to another location to reach the destination the mode of transport will be preferred by the passengers that maybe bicycle, car, bike, bus, train, etc. Due to the economic growth dependency of transportation, the location accessible cities will show higher the cost for transportation due to the huge traffic which limits the welfare of the society thus in terms of air pollution and using large amounts of land to travel. Detained issues such as robbery, pickpocket, mass killing which is a part of terrorism effects all together threatens the people who are in travel in order to contribute for these circumstances and enhance the security level smart transportation systems (STS) will be recommended. Around 60% of the people are living in cities and it will be increased to 65% by 2040. Around 1.3 million people who are in cities are driving their vehicles and roaming on the roadside area, with the support of wireless network radio technology and sensors all together brings many smart city projects and its implementation. The overall population is getting shortened due to the traffic crash on the road and it reflects the cost of 3% in the country’s GDP. It has to be taken care of to manage the natural environment and reduce accidents. There should be a realistic solution. One such supportive solution is the Intelligent Transportation System.

## 2.2 Overview of Its

Fast computing for vast operations is essential in day-to-day life. Today’s communication technology has proven intelligence in transportation with respect to safety and smart moves. The roadway accidents are getting reduced and provide a smart way to make a move of transportation even in traffic areas \[[2](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_2)\]. Vehicles are moving like water flow because of smart transport systems and traffic management. Operating and managing the transportation system’s safety and security is a big challenge but still achievable in the fast-growing number of vehicles world. By managing the traffic on the road and due to the toll gate, parking systems are extremely useful to avoid high-speed traveling for the case of emergency time to report. Traffic system structure and its efficiency has to be concentrated if there is an explosive number of four wheelers and two wheelers on the road. If that happens then the speed of vehicle movement is increasing from 15% to 20% and there is a possibility for fuel consumption also. It further decreases the death rate. Severe injuries due to the road crash is mainly due to the mobile phone’s usage, not wearing the helmets, over speed, etc. As per the global status report the, road safety is based on road infrastructure and traffic management system.

### 2.2.1 Recent Study on Its Implementation

1.  The entire public bus information is available to the passengers who want to get into the bus the information such as bus timings, current location, seat availability, etc. It is in the city of Glasgow belongs to Scotland.
2.  The recent design in which the routes of night bus services will be addressed based on the mobile phone location of the accessible users and provide the service based on the level of demand. It has been launched by the Seoul government.
3.  Optimized ambulance location based on the emergency call pattern in the city of New Orleans.
4.  Driverless cars arrive on streets to reach the destined location autonomously planned and in progress by Sydney metro.

### 2.2.2 Applications of Its

The growing number of population and the use of vehicles on the road makes slower speeds, long trip times, and increased vehicular queueing. As a whole the substantial increase of vehicles causes extreme traffic congestion. In the urban area, the predominant road facilities are the case for traffic collision for any mode of transport. ITS is a platform that supports the enhancement of quality and reaches the outcome in a controllable manner. Some of the applications of intelligent transportation systems (ITS) are efficient parking barrier management, electronic toll collection, emergency vehicle notification system, dynamic traffic light sequence, collision avoidance system, speed alerts, and traffic management. The information and communication technology like the internet of things (IoT) paves a way for efficient traffic management through the sensors where the real time data is fetched, processed, and transmitted. Further, it leads to avoid accidents, autonomous parking space identification in both rural and urban areas and also optimize the use of public city transport.

The most important application of intelligent transport systems are traffic control and management. There are many reasons for the traffic congestion. The traffic incidents such as work zones, special occasions, weather conditions are the primary factor that affects people’s time.

#### 2.2.2.1 Traffic Control and Management

The crucial aspect of traffic management enhances the quality of a particular city. So, the basic infrastructure problem is resolved by the construction planning and implementation. The success of IoT and its machine design for traffic control is purely about Sensors and cloud-based platforms. To examine, gather, and mine the collected data is possible with IoT devices. Mohammed et al. \[[5](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_5)\] proposed an IoT-based system model in which the real-time traffic scenario is observed. The system architecture consists of four layers, namely sensing layer, network layer, service layer and application layer. The sensing layer which consists of sensors and many active devices will sense the vehicular data and the wi-fi-based microcontroller installed will forward the data to the service layer. The open-source IoT platform is used to the devices which are connected for communication via the process of storing and managing. The device system is installed on road intersections which holds message display units to deliver the traffic message to the end user then and there. The unusual road incidents are also intimated with the expectations of road clearance or alternate route suggestions.

#### 2.2.2.2 Emergency Vehicle Notification

An approach of notifying the traffic scenario for the emergency vehicle is mounted with several alert units on a roadway \[[6](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_6)\]. The signal emitted from the transmitting unit of the emergency vehicle like ambulance is received by the detector unit on a roadway it compares the received signal with the proximity threshold of that exceeds it further notified the non-emergency vehicles through the light glowness. Thus, this makes the other vehicles know about the emergency vehicle which is on the road. The light flashes towards the emergency vehicle direction. The communication is continued by transferring the alert signal to the next alert unit on a roadway and the relay unit. This kind of prior information about the vehicles via signal propagation helps to avoid the death rate. The alertness given for the drivers through this technique helps to clear the path and save the lives of the humans who have got stuck up in the traffic even in emergency conditions. The equipped lights and sirens employed on the roadways will help for vehicular navigation on time by alerting the non-emergency vehicles. Another approach is e-call, the autonomous location and position of the vehicle is notified to the emergency help centers in European countries. It is made possible with the onboard sensors like air bag sensors which are active when accidents are met. There is a push button which helps to make a call manually by the occupant inside the car using 112. The trapped location is identified using the satellite while the call is made. With this data, the trashed location will be reached by the rescue team to save the people in the vehicle. It is proven that the death rate of 4–8% is reduced in Finland using this approach.

#### 2.2.2.3 Efficient Parking Management System

According to a survey report the 30% of traffic is due to the time taken by the drivers and motorists to find the spot for parking the vehicles. Most of the people consider this as the most frustrating activity and time consuming while dealing with the vehicle in urban cities. It can be made autonomous and easy with the help of an intelligent parking system which obviously falls under the category of intelligent transportation system \[[4](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_4)\]. The sensors and cameras are the major instruments to gather data in real time to measure parking occupancy and its status. Artificial Intelligence (AI) provision is for analyzing a wide variety of data like frequency of future vehicular traffic, Traffic in peak hours, type of vehicle, etc. The services like Advanced navigation services, knowledge about the traveling location, Traffic scenario, etc., and its data updating leads to the simplified functionalities which can be achieved with the help of AI. One of the AI-based systems is ALPR (automated license plate recognition) \[[9](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_9)\] which detects the number plate of the vehicles using 360° camera and optical character recognition patterns and then automatically frees up the space for parking of that vehicle without manual intervention. It is user friendly and in vehicle application software which consolidates the information of where the car is and how long it has been there and all other details. This detailed data of vehicular parking helps to find the vehicle if someone steals it. The major advantage through this is that the inconvenience of commuters gets eliminated, Security enforcement, Reduction of Fuel consumption through avoiding the unnecessary wastage of time while finding the free space and it supports the parking operator to provide efficient space for the incoming vehicles. Human assistance along with machine intelligence provides the efficient parking management system. The future focus towards the parking management is using AI-based robots for parking the vehicles if the user closes to the destination. It is a proven solution to limit the operating cost and the human workload of parking managers, parking patrol and the parking users even as the expanded technology is in use.

The IoT devices and several sensors help for the cloud-based location information updation. The IoT devices like Raspberry Pi, Arduino for parking occupancy detection using camera-based IoT systems. The traffic surveillance is done with the help of cloud data processing using several algorithms in an online manner for transmitting the data to the server database for parking allotment when can be in street and off street. For fast computing, multiple sensors like magnetic, ultrasonic, infrared, and loop sensors are used to detect multiple parking spaces. The parking occupancy status is efficiently determined using the IoT-based system.

##### 2.2.2.3.1 Parking Management

Uses various sensors on the road to minimize the traffic via intimating the real time situation to the vehicles using radio communication. In this way, the parking lot situation is also known prior using sensory network communication \[[4](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_4)\].

#### 2.2.2.4 Electronic Toll Collection System

Electronic toll collection System is the fastest alternative to manual toll collection \[[3](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_3)\]; It collects toll fees automatically when the vehicles cross the toll road, toll bridge or toll tunnel. In traditional toll collection methods, the driver needs to stop and pay the toll fee manually by cash or card. Electronic toll collection performs the various automated tasks in a sequential way; they are vehicle detection, vehicle classification, transaction processing and violation enforcement. Automated vehicle detection systems identify the passages of vehicles in toll. Once the vehicle gets detected, the vehicle classification system identifies the category of vehicle such as car, bus, truck, etc., then the transaction processing system proceeds the payment procedure. If the transaction completed successfully, then the vehicle was allowed to cross the toll. Also, is there any violation in the toll fee payment, violation enforcement system that detects the number plate of a vehicle.

##### 2.2.2.4.1 Freeway Traffic Management System

The collection of various information using vehicle detection sensors CCTV, the number of vehicles moving around will be calculated and recorded. The tollgate traffic management is getting accelerated with the help of fast cash transfer known as a high pass system which avoids the waiting time at the toll gate \[[3](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_3)\]. Thus, it helps to minimize the waiting time in toll gates.

#### 2.2.2.5 Bus Information System

It collects the location information of the bus and processes it via the information management system where the centralized information about the bus, its route, arrival time is viewable by the people who are standing in the bus stop which obviously reduces the tension of catching the bus and gives personal satisfaction also \[[1](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_1)\]. It is a kind of system implemented in Korea.

#### 2.2.2.6 Automatic Fare Collection

One card is designed to integrate all public transport, such as trains, taxis, buses, and its information \[[11](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_11)\]. The fare collection is automatically done with the help of that card which makes use of passengers to feel happy and satisfied about the payment.

#### 2.2.2.7 Speed Alert System

Nowadays road accidents increase due to high vehicle population and over speed. Nearly 1.25 million people die each year due to road traffic accidents \[[8](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_8)\]; it can be avoided by controlling the speed of a vehicle. Speed alert system gives alert to the passenger and driver when the vehicle speed exceeds the predefined limit. Speed and GPS coordinates of a vehicle calculated frequently, and it can be monitored easily from the traffic control room \[[7](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_7)\]. If the speed reaches the abnormal limit, then the buzzer alert given to the driver for attaining back to the normal speed. When the speed is not controlled within the specified limit the vehicle information such as speed, GPS position, vehicle number, vehicle color is sent to the traffic control officers through short message service (SMS).

#### 2.2.2.8 Temperature Monitoring

There is a necessity for monitoring the temperature for the temperaturecontrolled goods that carry huge loads. The container which carries the loads like meat, foods, and medicines have to be now in a particular temperature. In some cases, the customers are in need of temperature history at the time of delivery of products. This type of control avoids the decaying of goods which is purely dependent on temperature via updating the temperature deviation prior to the industry. EN 12830:1999 logger defines the standard for temperature monitoring.

The temperature monitoring setup consists of two sensors one is for inlet air temperature monitoring and another one is return air. Inlet air is to concentrate on what temperature is to be added to the goods whereas return air concentrates on how much temperature will it sustain in case of rise and fall. The cold transport is well regulated with the alarm system. The minimum and maximum threshold set up in the transported products helps to identify the suspicious temperature deviation and intimates that to the monitoring unit immediately to avoid the damage and penalties. The components that are essential for the tracking of goods temperature is by having temperature sensors, loggers in the cooling unit, on board computers to read the value from the sensor. In addition to the temperature updates to the web office it can be updated directly to the driver via the driver interface application in a mobile phone. Does the sensitive goods are safeguarded with the help of this efficient IoT temperature monitoring system \[[12](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_12)\].

## 2.3 Role of Iot in Transportation Sector

STS are integrated with the IoT in a stronger way and IoT provides the solution and new direction for the problems which arise in the smart transportation sector. In critical situations, IoT enabled STS to find the optimized and safer routes for travel. A major portion of transport sector revenue is depending on this smart transportation. IoT-adapted STS helps in traffic monitoring, traffic control, smart parking, automatic toll collection, etc. All this application uses a wide variety of sensors, and it paves a way for traffic surveillance and monitoring. There are two main factors that need to be discussed while considering the sensors. They are accuracy and reliability. Accuracy is measured by the number of vehicles counted more than one time as well as the number of vehicles missed in counting and reliability deals with a stable average vehicle count. Sensors are being deployed on the road, in vehicle and on-body sensors on drivers.

### 2.3.1 On-Road Sensors

On-Road Sensors is deployed on the road surface and it captures the information regarding traffic flow and helps in travel route prediction. It has high vehicle detection accuracy, and it also uses an effective fault tolerance mechanism \[[13](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_13)\]. In case any sensor fails while working, to manage that “backup sensors” are deployed on the road. There are various on-road sensors such as Pneumatic Road tube sensor, Inductive loop detector, Magnetic sensor, Piezoelectric sensor, Radar sensor, IR sensors, Ultrasonic sensors, and Road surface condition sensor. The role of road sensors such as: (i) pneumatic road tube sensors are used to track and count the number of vehicles; (ii) inductive loop detector used for absorbing the vehicle presence and its movement; (iii) magnetic sensor used to identify the status of stop and moving vehicles; (iv) piezoelectric sensor is to classify the vehicles and measures the vehicles speed and weight; (v) a RADAR sensor helps to measure the volume and speed of the vehicle along with its motion direction; (vi) IR sensors used for detection of pedestrians in crosswalks; (vii) ultrasonic sensors are for detecting the traffic congestion by sensing the nearby vehicles; and (viii) road surface condition sensor senses the strength of the road construction. Also, on-road sensors have the disadvantage of high sensor installation cost, and traffic interruption during repair, maintenance, installation, etc.

#### 2.3.1.1 Pneumatic Road Tube Sensor

Pneumatic road tube sensor most popularly used in vehicle sensing technology because of its portability it tracks and counts the number of vehicles on the road in an easy way. It is installed below the road surface \[[14](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_14)\]. In heavy traffic, this helps to sense and count the number of vehicles, speed of a vehicle, vehicle moving direction, gaps between the vehicles, etc. Road tube made up of synthetic polymer material or natural isoprene. Synthetic polymer material is more robust and suitable for all types of environments and natural isoprene is used only for colder climates. Pneumatic road tube sensors operate on the temperature between 15C and 60°C and handle the pressure between 3 and 8 barg. Pneumatic road tubes are used in two forms:

1.  **Single Pneumatic Road Tubes:** Counts the number of vehicles crossing the rubber tube as well as the gap between the vehicles.
2.  **Combination of Two Pneumatic Road Tubes:** Finds the direction of the traffic and vehicle moving speed.

In [Figure 2.1](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_1), a single pneumatic road tube is placed on the road surface which gathers the vehicle information.

![Figure 2.1 Single pneumatic road tube.](images/fig2_1_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.1 Single pneumatic road tube.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_1)

Air filled rubber tube placed across the road surface, one end of the tube is fully sealed, and the other end of the tube is connected with an Information processing unit. Pneumatic pressure sensors placed inside the rubber tube for sensing the vehicles when a pair of vehicle tiers cross over the tube air pressure inside the rubber tube gets increased. It closes the air switch and generates the electrical energy, and it sends the vehicle related information such as count, speed, vehicle direction, etc., to the Information Processing Unit. Road tubes are sustainable for all sorts of weather conditions and the life span of road tubes varies based on the factor’s location, installation, and volume of traffic. Also, it has the problem that a separate road tube is needed for each direction and the collected information needs to be processed or stored in a computer on the roadside. Road tubes are not suitable for higher volume roads and only work well for minimum duration on low volume roads.

#### 2.3.1.2 Inductive Loop Detector for Traffic Management

Inductive loop detector is an electromagnetic vehicle detection sensor used in traffic management that detects the vehicle which is crossing/arriving at the specified area of installation \[[15](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_15)\]. Electric current is induced in loop wire by the alternate current or moving magnet which detects the vehicle. Vehicles passing the loop are detected by the loop detector transmitted signals. Loops of wires are placed in the pavement of the road surface and the electronic amplifier is connected with both the ends of the wire. [Figures 2.2(a)](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_2a) and [2.2(b)](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_2b) shows the inductive loop detector installed surface and inductive loop detector working, respectively, if the vehicle crosses through the magnetic field of a loop its magnetic field gets affected and this disturbance is sensed by the amplifier and the vehicle gets detected.

 ![Figure 2.2a Inductive loop detector installed surface.](images/fig2_2a_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.2a Inductive loop detector installed surface.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_2a)

![Figure 2.2b Inductive loop detector working.](images/fig2_2b_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.2b Inductive loop detector working.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_2b)

The electronic loop detector unit transmits energy into the inductive wire loops at frequencies between 10 kHz and 200 kHz. The inductance gets reduced when the vehicle passes over the loop then the vehicle gets detected. It is also having low manufacturing and transportation costs compared to others. Inductive loop detector size varies by 5 feet to 6 feet, and it takes the installation time between minimum 10 minutes to one hour based on road condition. Most of the conventional loop detectors are used to find the vehicle presence, count the number of vehicles crossing the loop, speed, and gap between vehicles. Nowadays inductive loop detectors come up with advanced technology to classify vehicles. The major drawback of this sensor is at every phase of road maintenance it needs to be reinstalled. Also, traffic disturbance will happen at installation time.

#### 2.3.1.3 Magnetic Sensors for Detecting Vehicle Presence

Magnetic sensors sense and detect the vehicle presence in the sensor installed surface. It distinguishes the vehicles and allows us to make decisions on traffic light control \[[16](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_16)\]. Also, it gives the improved solution for heavy traffic based on the sensed data. Magnetic sensors can be placed on the middle or side of the road, when the vehicle or metal object crosses the sensor, it affects the earth’s magnetic field, and the metal object or vehicle gets detected. [Figure 2.3](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_3) depicts the magnetic sensor embedded integrated circuit (IC) which shows the magnetic field distortion by the vehicle. Magnetic sensors are very small in size therefore it has reduced the installation issues and cost.

 ![Figure 2.3  Magnetic field distortion by the vehicle sensed by magnetic sensor.](images/fig2_3_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.3  Magnetic field distortion by the vehicle sensed by magnetic sensor.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_3)

#### 2.3.1.4 Piezoelectric Sensors To Count the Roadside Vehicle Count

Piezoelectric Sensors use the piezoelectric effect and identify the changes in pressure, temperature, and force on the sensor environment. Piezoelectric sensors kept under the road surface in a diagonal position \[[16](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_16)\]. Once the mechanical stress is applied to the sensor piezoelectric effect generates the electricity. When each tier crosses the sensor installed area that changes the voltage pulse. A number of vehicles crossing the sensor is counted by the change in voltage. To count this voltage, a change counting device is connected with the sensor and placed on the roadside. Piezoelectric sensors are made up of quartz material and have high flexibility with small size. This sort of sensor operates on the temperature between 20°C and 60°C and having the sensitivity of 5 V/µƐ. Piezoelectric sensors do not require any external voltage. Once the pressure is applied to it creates a charge and detects the vehicles with the vehicle passing speed of 112 km/h along with weight.

#### 2.3.1.5 Radar Sensor for Measuring the Vehicle Speed

Radar sensor represented in [Figure 2.4](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_4) identifies the objects using the microwave radiation. The transmitted low energy microwave hits the vehicle and is reflected by the object; while reflecting it returns location and speed of a vehicle also it helps in traffic monitoring and protect from vehicle crashes \[[16](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_16)\]. A RADAR sensor helps to measure the volume and speed of the vehicle along with its motion direction. This sort of sensor is mainly classified into different categories like 24 GHz and 60 GHz based on its range. Object detection technology of 24 GHz and 60 GHz, respectively, detects objects within the range of 100 m and 10 m. Autonomous cars speed is controlled by this radar sensor; in the varying environmental conditions, speed gets slowed down also the sensor will work effectively. Radar sensors will not be affected by the weather conditions like rainy, windy, foggy, etc. It is more suitable for outdoor applications also due its withstanding physical surface hardness.

![Figure 2.4 Radar sensor object detection.](images/fig2_4_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.4 Radar sensor object detection.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_4)

#### 2.3.1.6 Infrared Sensor (Ir) for Pedestrian Detection

Infrared sensor (IR) detects the people’s presence using infrared radiation in the surrounding environment. It is simply called an optical sensor. IR sensor used for the detection of pedestrians in crosswalks \[[16](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_16)\]. The light beam gets interrupted and breaks the continuity of it if any of the objects crosses over it. It prevents the unknown hits on the people who are walking on the roadside. The IR sensor detects the object as shown in [Figure 2.5](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_5) with the maximum sensing range of 200 meters. The IR sensor requires the mounting structure for both transmitter and receiver end for the stability and constant working without any disturbance.

![Figure 2.5 Infrared sensor object detection.](images/fig2_5_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.5 Infrared sensor object detection.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_5)

#### 2.3.1.7 Ultrasonic Sensor for Estimating Traffic Flow

Ultrasonic sensors are for detecting the traffic congestion by sensing the nearby vehicles; it measures the distance between it and other objects which are on its line of sound \[[16](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_16)\]. With the echo from the object, the distance is estimated as shown in [Figure 2.6](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_6). The maximum sensing range of the ultrasonic sensor is 4 meters. The road surface is considered as a fixed and stationary object whereas the vehicles are considered as the moving object. The sensor is employed 4 m from the road surface and positioned in mid periodically along the vertical alignment. If any of the vehicles crosses this area, then the distance between the sensor and the road surface is getting reduced, thus ensuring the vehicle presence and its occupancy period to estimate the traffic flow. Here the length of the vehicle is fed as an input accordingly to calculate the speed of the vehicles moved along the line of sound of an ultrasonic sensor and the count of it is acquired for an intelligent transportation system. The traffic information is estimated in this way using an ultrasonic sensor.

![Figure 2.6 Ultrasonic sensor for vehicle detection.](images/fig2_6_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.6 Ultrasonic sensor for vehicle detection.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_6)

The sensors mounted on both the sides of the roads will enhance the efficiency of detecting the vehicle over a single side mounting device. The sensor network configuration is quite challenging for the poor road infrastructure. The horizontal placement of sensors will register the nonvehicles in a vehicle category and provide the data in an inappropriate manner. It happens at times and can be eradicated by placing the sensors on the edge of the roads. The road surface has to be smooth to avoid the case of absorption and dispersion of sound signal emitted from the ultrasonic sensor. For accurate traffic flow estimation and avoid congestions, the sensors are overhead mounted for covering wider road networks. HCSR04 is the one which is applicable to measure the object which is at a distance from 2 cm to 400 cm. The ultrasonic sensors can be mounted on the parking slots for detecting the vehicles to ensure the slot freeness; thus it is possible for efficient parking management also. The ultrasonic sensor is used to detect the disorder in the road based on the decayed reflection intensity of ultrasonic waves. It produces alarm sounds in case of dangerous road conditions. It saves more lives of the human with its easy configuration and availability \[[17](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_17)\].

#### 2.3.1.8 Road Surface Condition Sensor

Road surface condition sensor senses the weather condition and strength of the road construction \[[16](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_16)\]. The abnormalities of the road while traveling is not observed all the time by the drivers, especially during night times. This leads to many accidents and complications at the driver’s end. The road surface condition sensor is used to sense and assess the pavement surfaces, roads, highways, bridges, and parking areas. It also supports the elderly people and the visually impaired people to know the road surface by prior alertness.

There are three configurations.

*   The sensor is mounted on the pavement surface with the drilled cavity even though working to face the huge traffic scenario in real-time;
*   Non-invasive pole mounted installation to measure the road surface; and
*   The real time surface condition sensing is with remote sensing via the sensor mounted on the vehicle.

Puddle, asphalt, and lawn or soil are the types of road condition. The acceptable form of road condition is asphalt because the chance for getting slip and fall is more possible for the aged and visually affected people in puddles. This way of distinction is very helpful and supportive for the physically handicapped people who are coming in wheelchairs also.

AI enabled features of all the above road sensors will observe the road condition and accurately collect data that is driven and analyzed for ground transportation in a better way.

### 2.3.2 In-Vehicle Sensors

In vehicle sensors are used to observe the surrounding environment while driving it helps for the driver to ensure safety. Various in-vehicle sensors are shown in [Figure 2.7](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_7). The sophistication of IoT is estimated with the help of sensors and its integration unit.

![Figure 2.7 Sensors placed inside car.](images/fig2_7_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.7 Sensors placed inside car.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_7)

#### 2.3.2.1 Night Vision Sensor

Major accidents happen unknowingly because of the less vision at the driver’s end. Human vision is quite poor at nighttime when compared to the animal’s vision. It is made possible with the help of appropriate electromagnetic spectrum and light intensity. To see the person’s image even at a distance the thermographic cameras are used \[[19](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_19)\]. It is an additional option embedded in the advanced vehicles. The IR and thermal sensor are mounted in the vehicle and controlled with the onboard computers employed on the center console. The object detection and its distance are sensed with the help of IR and displayed in the display device which helps to circumvent the obstacle in front of the vehicle sooner before reaching it. The sensors can be placed anywhere in the vehicle like windshield, headlights, etc.

There are two categories involved in night vision sensors. They are:

1.  **Active Night Vision Sensor:** It is a sensor supported to know about the objects which are viewable around 600 feet distance with the help of IR sensors. One of the quiet drawbacks is that the weather condition affects the sensor’s capability of visualizing.
2.  **Passive Night Vision Sensor:** In passive night vision sensors, there will not be any clear image of Infront objects but still supports for the extended range of 1,000 feet. It purely depends on the thermographic camera, and it is unreliable if the temperature is high.

#### 2.3.2.2 Speed Sensor

It is the sensor which is used to read the speed of the vehicle using wheel rotation. It is usually an optical sensor in which the light signal along with its reflection is considered \[[20](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_20)\]; Inductive sensor in which the magnetic pulse is getting generated and is in the form of a wave;

The efficiency of the operating functionalities of the vehicles is reflected by using speed sensors for Automotive vehicles.

There are various aspects to consider to be more useful. Some of them are:

*   The sensor input helps to control the pressures on the power steering to slow down the speed in case of parking areas.
*   The anti-lock braking system which is simply called ABS utilizes the speed sensor data and helps to identify when the wheel gets locked up and releases a certain amount of pressure to maintain the stability with respect to the direction of the vehicle it is moving.
*   The electronic engine control systems also will make use of vehicle speed sensor data.

As a whole, it helps to find the fault which occurs due to uncontrollable speed via proper signaling from a speed sensor. It is deployed in the wheel to measure and compare the wheel rotations and take necessary actions further with the sensed data. The data is given to the ECU unit to instruct the exact timing of when the gear has to be changed. Since the automatic transmission shift sensor and speed sensor are having the correlated functionalities, the fault of the vehicle is also easily determined.

#### 2.3.2.3 Proximity Sensor

The nearby and closest object is detected with the help of a proximity sensor. The sensed measurements are highly useful for Automatic Cruise Control. The speed of the vehicle is purely based on the vehicles in front and behind on it \[[20](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_20)\]. It is very much useful for the driverless cars to take necessary actions with the detected signal. There are different types of proximity sensors. They are inductive, capacitive, and photoelectric. (i) The electromagnetic field generated from an inductive type of proximity sensor is changed, if the metallic target is entering into the field which reflects a varying proportion of the field. (ii) The capacitive type is not only for metal targets but also for other forms like glass, liquid, etc. (iii) Through the breaks in the beam of light between sender and receiver the targets and their presence are detected with the help of photoelectric proximity sensor.

The most common forms are magnetic and ultrasonic proximity sensors.

#### 2.3.2.4 Air Bag

The airbag sensor is a passive protection device where the human interruption is not required and designed only for the cars, not for the motorcycles. The signals from the air bag sensor which is a MEMS accelerometer rely on the control unit which enables the safety features when at risk \[[21](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_21)\]. There are two common forms of air bag sensor one is electrical which is purely a battery operated and another one is mechanical which works independent of electrical energy source. The generic ball and tube mechanism is an electromechanical one which depicts the automotive reaction while facing the collision. The tube consists of a small electric circuit switch and the ball/roller type weighted coil is attached together with a magnet. If the accident occurs, then the ball is dispatched and gets rolled in the forward direction which hits the switch and makes the circuit to be closed to activate seat belt locks and automatic door locks, etc. At the same time, the inflator system gets the signal and starts to fill up the nitrogen gas through chemical charging and explode it as an aid. The impact of the sensed data is analyzed with the help of AI crash algorithms. The crash algorithm designed for the electronic control unit of Airbags prevents the vehicle occupants by enabling the number of airbags to be busted at that point of time. The readiness and the response time is just a few milliseconds.

#### 2.3.2.5 Pressure Sensor

The pressure sensor is used to sense useful vehicle fluids like gearbox and engine oil, braking system’s hydraulic oil, oil in cooling unit and fuel system oil. It is based on a piezo resistive effect in which the change in resistance causes deflection due to the fluid pressure. The targeting features make the sensor acceptable to do the designed task only when withstanding the high temperature, pressure, dust air environments at the extreme end \[[22](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_22)\]. The sensing ability of a pressure sensor is from 0 bar to 600 bar with the operating temperature of –40°C to +125°C. Recent inventions on pressure sensors are highly useful to circumvent from the situations of pedestrian hits to be normal by giving the forceful airy barrier if they hit in the front face of the vehicle. It is by sensing the deformity of the bumper as soon as the pedestrian slides on it and enables the safety system of the car via activating the reserved compressed air in the bay.

#### 2.3.2.6 Gas Composition Sensor

The air quality is observed with the help of gas composition sensors \[[23](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_23)\]. The gasses like carbon monoxide (CO) and oxygen are sensed with the help of gas sensors. If in case the dangerous gas concentration is exhausted the limit, then it will be alerted to the driver via the alarm sound. The uniformity of ventilation and air conditioning is maintained by continuous monitoring of the gas present in the circulating air of the vehicle surrounding the environment. The sensor should leak proof and water resistant to operate from – 40°C to 85°C. It tends to support vehicular fuel efficiency.

#### 2.3.2.7 Temperature Sensor

Temperature sensors are in need for the cold transport systems such as food transportation using the container milk, meat, and medicines; all need to be maintained in a particular temperature to prevent the items from decaying. The in-car temperature sensor is a negative temperature coefficient sensor in which the resistance value decreases when the temperature increases \[[18](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_18)\]. The signal voltage acceptable for this temperature sensor is 0 volt and 5 volts. It will sense the vehicle into two verticals one is in the passenger compartment and another one is air temperature outside of the vehicle. The temperature sensor is acting as an input for many units available in the car such as fan speed, cooling loads, Sun loads which are adjustable based on the varying temperature. LM35 is the temperature sensor used here which has three leads (power supply, ground, and output voltage) to measure the ambient temperature. ESP 8266 Wi-Fi module used to feed the temperature sensor’s data to the web. The battery lifetime of the sensor plays a vital role for the sensor working. There is a wireless battery free sensor called RFM 3200 which has a longer lifetime compared to others. The advantages are the complete eradication of nonlinear temperature problems through which it ensures high accuracy.

#### 2.3.2.8 Position Sensor

The safety requirements for high end vehicles are ensured by detecting the abnormalities in a rapid fashion through the sensor technology. The power steering electric cars are in need of calculating the position to decrease the fuel consumption and CO[2] emissions. To detect the angular rotation of the steering wheel and control the motor, the position sensor is deployed in the vehicle \[[24](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_24)\]. The precise controlling of torque both at starting and highspeed operations is possible by fixing the position sensors in electrically commutated motors.

There are three types of position sensors. They are hall effect sensors, optical sensors, and inductive position sensors:

1.  **Hall Effect Sensor:** It is used to sense the position of the pedal. It detects the effect of resistance on the current carrying conductor with respect to the magnetic field applied. The IC-based AS5262 is a Hall-based position sensor that produces 360° angular measurements accurately. Its operating temperature is – 40°C to 150°C. It is more immune to unnecessary stray fields via proper surface magnetization.
2.  **Optical Sensor:** It consists of phototransistors to sense the movement of optical marks. It is a crankshaft position sensor for sensing the light being reflected from the shaft’s surface which is in rotation. The change can be sensed with the light and dark marks of the optical beam.
3.  **Inductive Pedal Position Sensor:** The digital component is used to drive the coil with high current. The consecutive creation of the magnetic field and its relative current phase is used to determine the position of the rotating coil using the accelerator pedal position sensor.

#### 2.3.2.9 Humidity Sensor

It is an electronic device known as a sensor which senses the surrounding environment’s air humidity which could form mist \[[25](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_25)\]. If the sensed amount of water vapor present in the air (mist) quantity on the glass of the vehicle is considered to be more then it will be difficult for the people to see the objects which are in front of the vehicles. The IoT intelligence is proven by integrating one sensor’s data with the other sensor to react and thus leads to optimization. The air humidity sensor data is given to the temperature sensor to measure the temperature at which the concentration of humidity is high. So, with the help of a climate control unit the dew point at which temperature the mist becomes high is calculated and regulates it with a condenser of the air conditioning unit present inside the vehicle. The major advantage of this battery enabled sensor is to make the people inside the vehicles drive in a comfortable way through its features like durability of 15 years, small size, reliability, etc. It further leads to avoiding accidents in the hilly areas majorly.

#### 2.3.2.10 Fog Prediction Sensor

The self-driving vehicles and as well as the human driving vehicles are facing difficulty in the poor weather condition like morning fog. The fog sensor senses the level of moisture content lying on the glass surface inside the vehicle \[[26](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_26)\]. The fog sensor which is in small size will generate the infrared light on its own to detect the presence of fog. The detection is done with the help of the scattering phenomenon of the light beam. The scattered beam of emitted IR is again received by a fog sensor if the fog is formed in the interior glass. The observed data from the sensor is fed to the climate control unit present inside the vehicle and defog it with the help of any heating effect. The fog sensor is highly accurate and detect it before it is being visualized by the human.

#### 2.3.2.11 Vehicle Crash Sensor

There are many blind spots which are not viewable for the driver if they are in the driver’s seat. There are various sensors which are useful to detect the obstacle before an accident occurs \[[27](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_27)\]. Electromagnetic sensors, Ultrasonic sensors are the few sensors useful to detect the obstacle and alert the driver when the vehicle is on the move.

*   Using an electromagnetic sensor employed in the bumper will emit the signal to indicate the obstacle which is nearby to the vehicle. If the vehicle is continually maintained at the same speed without noticing the emitted signal indication of the sensor, then the sound signal is produced to alert the driver. This process is considered to be a smooth and slow process.
*   Using the wireless ultrasonic sensor, the distance of the obstacle is detected by the echo sound of the generated radio signal from the obstacle. It can be placed in the front and as well as back parts of the vehicle.
*   Reverse car parking sensors will be activated if the car is in reverse gear mode. The sensor will generate ultrasonic radio waves to transmit and receive the reflected signals from the obstacle.

### 2.3.3 On Body Sensors

The drivers are spending most of their lifetime in the vehicles and their driving manner brings the happiness for many people for reaching the destination on time. Still the continuous workloads and their competency to drive the vehicles on a timely basis makes them run continuously and induces some sort of aggressiveness. Alcohol consumption, injected drugs, drowsiness, inattention driving, speediness are the major causes for the vehicle crash and people death due to those accidents. Due to this the traffic safety is also affected and put in danger. It has to be taken care of by continuously observing the behavioral actions of the driver and ensures whether the driver is driving the vehicle with proper control and stability \[[28](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_28)\]. The sensed physiological signs help to avoid accidents happening due to the inadequate sensible actions of the driver.

The emotional disorders are determined with the help of clues which have been programmed in a sensing unit. The role of on body sensors such as EOG, ECG, EEG, EMG senses the abnormalities in driver’s health conditions and helps to ensure safe traveling. On-body sensors are placed in various parts of the human body are shown in [Figure 2.8](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_8) and it detects driver distraction, drowsiness, emotions using monitoring units. The frequency of yawning, eye blinking, head movement is predicted with the help of a combination of sensors. The EOG (electrooculogram) sensor is used to detect the eyeball movement; It detects the eye blinks up to 10 cm range. EMG is used to detect the skin’s electric potential; EEG (electroencephalogram) sensor is for observing the brain activity of the driver which consists of electrodes to analyze the alpha, beta, and gamma waves induced in the brain stimuli to detect the drowsiness and fatigue of the driver. This helps to provide safety to both drivers and passengers. The heart rate of the driver is picked up for periodic intervals of time by using an ECG sensor. It consists of two electrodes employed on the body which is in contact with the driver’s skin directly but not invasive. There is an option to make it non-contact by positioning it in the driver’s seat back also. Because it is possible to get the ECG and EEG signal even with their hair or cloth without the skin contact \[[28](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_28)\].

 ![Figure 2.8 On body sensors.](images/fig2_8_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.8 On body sensors.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_8)

The variance in electrical activity and irregularity in the sensed heartbeat signal shows that the driver is quite inactive through drowsiness. The signal acquisition module and the signal conditioning unit which holds the filter circuit to remove the unwanted signal and amplifier circuit to strengthen the signal will help to reach the signal processing unit through wireless communication. This further leads to making the driver fall asleep using proper alertness.

Alcohol detection – using the PAS 328 alcohol sensor the amount of alcohol consumption is detected using the breathing. This setup is connected to the fuel supply unit which cuts down the fuel supply to stop the vehicle. If the 10% of alcohol is detected, then the vehicle will automatically be directed to the speed of 80 km/hr. If it is between 20% and 30% the speed of the vehicle is maintained to be 60 km/hr. If it exceeds 30%, then the alert message is triggered and displayed in the controller unit LCD screen. In case the message is ignored and does not stop the vehicle within a few minutes then it directly passes the short message along with the location of the driver using GPS to the regulatory commission which takes care of this.

## 2.4 Role of Ai in Transportation

One such way to incorporate human intelligence in machines is known to be AI. The conceptual contribution of AI impacts the transport sector widely. The AI technology deliberately contributes to passenger safety, reducing traffic congestion and accidents. The important considerations of AI are safety, efficiency, reliability, and pollution. To make the intelligence of transportation a bit higher it will be embedded to have autonomous functionalities in buses, trains, cars, and marine vehicles. The fuel consumption is possible with the help of indication about the road traffic status in prior while opting the route to reach destination. Advancements of it are proven while dispatching the loads carried by consecutive trucks are being in control of its accelerator and brake through AI is made possible even in case of complex traffic. The integration of IoT and AI administering the recent marketing trends to clear the traffic and maintaining the actions of vehicles in a smooth way.

In transportation AI technology it is mainly involved in self-driving vehicles, traffic light control and pedestrians path identification \[[29](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_29)\]. In urban areas, it can be used to predict the decision from the previously learned data and accidents can be avoided in a major way. In critical situations when the decision is unpredictable, AI methods like neural networks, genetic algorithms (GAs) can be used for prediction. In transportation, optimized routes are identified priorly. It reduces the transportation cost and provides safety.

1.  **AI in Public Transport:** In countries with high populations, public transportation becomes the greatest gift. In AI, machines act with human intelligence \[[29](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_29)\]. Deployment and need of AI are classified under different categories such as learning, prediction, and automation. In the learning stage opinion/feedback is collected from the public environment. During prediction, the transportation model identifies the congested route and provides the safe alternate path. Once the learning and prediction gets over, the entire process is automated for solving the forthcoming problems.
2.  **Autonomous Aircraft:** In autonomous airplanes at the time of boarding, to verify the identities of the passengers AI uses the facial recognition technology. Scanning processes are automated and the passengers can scan their face and it gets verified automatically without any human intervention. Also, at the time of climbing, airplanes will use the highest rate of fuel. To measure the fuel consumption level, a pilot climb profile is created along with flight information. AI models analyze the pilot’s profile and predict the expected fuel consumption priorly.
3.  **Goods Delivery:** To avoid the issues in goods transportation and delivery, drones with AI technology are deployed; it avoids the human error. Drones used for delivering the product in customer’s doorstep. Image processing and Neural Network technology used for detecting the objects/obstacles such as hills, buildings, etc., in the flying environment. Sensors and cameras are used for capturing images. Once the images get captured, it is processed, and some useful decisions are taken from this processed information.
4.  **Traffic Management:** In traffic management, AI creates the environment friendly methods for smooth transportation. Traffic lights are embedded with AI technology based on traffic congestion and it will take decisions automatically \[[29](#ch2.xhtml#ref2_29)\]. AI recognizes the environment and acts accordingly for taking decisions.

### 2.4.1 Implementation of Integrated Iot and Ai Technique for Driver Monitoring

The safety-enabling technology is successfully implemented with a test model in which the IoT-enabled sensor Network is configured and its output data is analyzed with the help of AI technique. The eye blink sensor consists of an IR transmitter and receiver detects the number of eye blinks using the reflected rays from the eyes. The sensed data from the eye blink sensor acts as a test data and is driven to the trained model to identify whether the driver is in a drowsy state or not. If the eye position of the driver remains closed for a specific period due to drowsiness, then it may be a cause for the accidents; this can be prevented by alerting the driver then and there in case of any lethargic driving.

In [Figures 2.9(a)](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_9) and [2.9(b)](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_9) in which the eyes are being opened and closed for some time. [Figure 2.9](#ch2.xhtml#fig2_9)(b) illustrates the intentional eye closure and indicates the drowsy feel direct to less concentration.

 ![Figure 2.9  (a) Driver's opened eyes; and (b) driver's closed eyes.](images/fig2_9_B.jpg)

[Figure 2.9  (a) Driver's opened eyes; and (b) driver's closed eyes.](#ch2.xhtml#R_fig2_9)

Using this model, the region of interest (RoI) is created with the help of detected faces which are then fed to the CNN classifier. It classifies the eyes based on open and closed condition with its obtained values. The normal person blinks the eye 10 times in a minute. According to this, the threshold is fixed based on the time which is 15 seconds. If the obtained value is exceeded the threshold value, then it confirms the driver’s state as drowsy and the alert sign will be indicated through an alarm signal and display notification to the driver.

The model provides the accurate detection and classification even in midnight condition. Thus, the model efficacy saves the passengers and ensures safe driving all the time.

## 2.5 Conclusion

The revolution of IoT and AI in the Intelligent Transportation System is reviewed with several IoT sensors and AI techniques. And structured the way of managing the transportation in three verticals as on road, in vehicle and on body of driver. The detailed role and significance of AI and IoT in ITS are highlighted and implemented as a model. It has CNN classifier which shows its readiness to classify the driver’s drowsiness feels quite an advantage for the sensor enabled network using IoT. Thus, the implemented model, its detection efficiency and the estimation time proves their intelligence for the ITS. Likewise, the probing of each and every thing in transportation leads to a better management of it autonomously.
