# Chapter 4 Various AI Models to Mitigate Transport Pollution

T. JANANI, P. SOBIYAA, K. SATHIS KUMAR, S. NAVEENA, and P. PONNILA

Bannari Amman Institute of Technology, Sathyamangalam, Tamil Nadu, India

## Abstract

In recent decades, vehicle usage increased enormously, and it made significant adverse effects on human health and the environment. Contaminants released by sources of transport cause major damage to the surroundings. Human action, especially the burning of fossil fuels that emit carbon dioxide (CO[2]), methane, and other greenhouse gases (GHGs) into the atmosphere, affects the environment. Due to the transport pollution earth’s temperature increases and that leads to global warming. Not all vehicles have similar effects. Transport pollution majorly occurs by burning the oil resources in the world and providing dangerous effects on the environment.

AI is making more changes in all kinds of sectors. Instead of mentioning it as changing, it is improving the current status of all technologies. AI shows a lot more improvement in the transport sector. The transport field has already adapted AI in several methods, and it provides autonomous functions for cars, trains, ships, and airplanes. Due to new methodologies of AI, even the traffic flow becomes smoother. AI-based autonomous transportation might minimize the human mistakes that cause so many traffic accidents.

People care about the water consumed by them but less care about the quality of the air they breathe. Day by day, the quality of air is being reduced due to the emission of pollutants. India’s capital Delhi is popular for politics, protest, and highly for its pollution. The Central Pollution Control Board predicts severe air pollutant presence in Delhi and neighboring cities. The air pollution issue in Delhi has become a serious concern as the air quality index (AQI) continues to worsen. At noon, the town reported an AQI of 381. In the mid-week in Delhi AQI will be on average of 395. Innovative AI systems are launched to predict the quality of air. Systems such as the air quality micro-climate monitoring system (MCMS) are used to measure the quality of air and provide uncompromising data to the Government as a valuable resource to take appropriate action from the provided data.

Artificial Neural Network has been used to predict the contaminants which are present in the air using methods like multilayer perceptron, extreme learning machines, echo state neural network, regression model, and genetic algorithm (GA).

Various other artificial neural networks (ANNs) mathematical simulation methods such as regression, multiple regression, and time series analysis have been used to measure the air quality, reduce traffic congestion and produce efficient data. By minimizing traffic, transport pollution problems are addressed in the maximum concern. AI not only aims to make human lives easier, but it also makes transportation safe, clean, smart, and even better. AI is growing up to be one of society’s most useful technologies. With it, we will begin to make major improvements to our soil, climate, and water and aircraft clean them up for a cleaner world.

## 4.1 Pollution

Pollution releases toxic, dangerous, unsafe substances into the environment and spoils its natural condition. Those substances are called pollutants. The pollutant may be in any form of gas, solid or liquid and some are natural pollutants like volcanic ash, hurricane dust, but the majority of the pollutants are created by human activities. Human beneficial product production is carried out in many industries and that causes heavy pollution in the environment.

The human activity involved in producing pollutions:

*   Trash;
*   Factory extracts;
*   Vehicle emissions;
*   Import-export activities through ships, airplanes, lorries, etc.

Humans pollute the environment in several ways. Vehicle usage has become huge because of the increasing population all around the world. Vehicles such as cars, motorbikes, buses, trucks need fossil fuel as intake to move from one place to another. Electricity is considered a safe and clean source of energy while generating electric power plants emit dangerous pollutants that severely affect human health. Most industries release waste into the air and water and those wastages cause damages to all living beings. Pesticides are used to fertilize the plants and to give more yield quickly. Pesticides have to be used at a very minimum rate but to prevent insects’ high toxic pesticides are used. When those fossil fuels are burned, it emits terrible pollutants ([Figure 4.1](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_1)).

[FIGURE 4.1 Pollutants.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_1)

  
| Pollutants |
| --- |
| 1\. Particular matter |
| --- |
| 2\. Volatile organic compounds |
| 3\. Carbon monoxide |
| 4\. Sulfur dioxide |
| 5\. Greenhouse gases |
| 6\. Nitrogen oxides |

Compared to the rural area, urban areas are more responsible for all kinds of pollution even pollution spreads to places where there are no lives. Pesticides and other chemical substances are being found in our soils present in Antarctic ice sheets. Plastics used by humans are not disposed of properly, resulting in the accumulation of plastics in the northern Pacific Ocean as a bunch. Not only it is contaminating the oceans with a long day-by-day air quality, but sea creatures are also consuming plastics and other waste substances as their food and losing their lives \[[3](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_3)\]. In factories during the manufacture of the products, toxic gases are released as the reaction of the process. Toxic gases mix into the air and spread all around the earth. When humans inhale the polluted air, the possibilities are very high to get a respiratory disease.

Every organism that lives in the world depends on the air, land, and water which is available as natural resources in the world. All kinds of organisms are affected when these resources are polluted.

## 4.2 Transport Pollution

One of the biggest challenges the universe is facing is pollution. There is a lot of pollution which the world is facing, among such one is transport pollution. In the modern era, automobiles have become of paramount importance to humankind. Everyday involves the use of vehicles by humans to reduce his traveling effort. For moving from one place to another transportation is involved which categories various modes like land, sea, or air. The most commonly used mode of transportation is by road which involves Two-wheelers, four-wheelers, and even Earthmovers.

Two-wheelers have become very important in the means of transportation where people use them for daily commuting to schools, colleges, offices, and business as well. Because of this frequent use on a large scale, two-wheeler manufacturing has increased a lot. The engines used in two-wheelers are 2 strokes and 4 strokes. Both stroke engines use fossil fuel which is extracted from earth mainly undergo various stages of refining which finally leads to the product like petrol and diesel which is the main source for automobile.

For transportation or moving goods from one place to another place on a large scale, humans are using heavy vehicles which mainly use fossil fuels as their source for running the engines. Day-to-day necessary items include food items, milk, and groceries mainly depend upon transportation as their factor to get moved from manufacturer to consumers. Because of modern civilization, humans started consuming foods from various countries which mainly needed goods to get transported in large numbers.

Trucks play a vital role in the movement of goods from one place to another place which uses internal combustion engines as their major source. These engines use petrol and diesel as their source and it emits carbon monoxide (CO) as residue after they get combusted. Not only does CO[2] properly exist. Day-by-day air quality sulfur dioxide (SO[2]) and nitrogen dioxide (NO[2]) which is very dangerous to humankind and nature as well. These gases cause harmful effects to lungs for human beings when it is inhaled, it blocks the transportation of oxygen to the brain \[[12](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_12)\].

Once these harmful gases are inhaled, it shortens the lifespan of humans by giving various side effects. Furthermore, these gases cause depletion in the ozone layer which in turn gives way for entering harmful ultraviolet radiation. This radiation causes various types of cancer to humankind. One such harmful disease is skin cancer. This radiation causes global warming on a large scale, and it will soon make the earth not fit for humans to live.

### 4.2.1 Impact of Transport Pollution in Air

Pollutants produced by burning petroleum products which are produced from automobiles, airplanes play a vital role in air transport pollution. Incomplete combustion of petrol and diesel, when mixed with air in the atmosphere, leads to the formation of harmful pollutants like CO, carbon dioxide, and NO[2]. The majority of this pollution is caused when the vehicle’s engine is left idle in ON condition in traffic.

Nowadays, all people own two-wheelers and four-wheelers for their use and commercial use, which leads to increases in pollution when vehicles stop during traffic signals, leading to more emission of pollutants. CO produced from engines will cause direct poisoning when inhaled. This leads to liver damage and even to cancer when exposed for a longer time.

On the other hand, greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions from airplanes and Jet planes cause serious emissions of CO[2]. The automobile and aerospace sector alone is not a major contributor to Air transport pollution. Major pollution is caused by emissions from Industries with a larger scale of pollutants, these pollutants will directly mix with air which reduces the Air quality majorly and leads to impure air \[[3](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_3)\]. The majority of industries including thermal power generation and steel manufacturing industries cause a major impact on Air transport pollution. These pollutants, when mixed with air, will cause acid rain and they will be mixed with normal rain and cause the worst effects on all living organisms.

#### 4.2.1.1 Acid Rain

Marble and other types of stone are also destroyed by acid rain. It has annihilated the words on gravestones and caused damage to several historic structures and monuments. Taj Mahal in Agra is well known for its white marble; it has become pale as a result of years of exposure to acid rain ([Figure 4.2](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_2)).

![Figure 4.2 Acid rain.](images/fig4_2_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.2 Acid rain.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_2)

Acid rain has been attempted to be avoided by producing energy that is required for human activity without the participation of fossil fuel. Some developed countries tried various methods to eradicate acid rain, but those methods would be a temporary solution. Renewable energy is the best alternative for air pollution because when used, it produces fewer pollutants when compared to non-renewable energy.

#### 4.2.1.2 Greenhouse Gases (GHGs)

Greenhouse gases (GHGs) have a major contribution to the current level of air pollution in the world. GHGs like Carbon dioxide, Methane, present in the atmosphere, and also those gases have benefits too. GHGs are not completely a dangerous gas that causes air pollution, in case some amount of GHGs are necessary for the atmosphere. Plants breathe Carbon dioxide. Mainly to maintain the atmosphere in a proper warmth condition GHGs are essential \[[21](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_21)\].

Sunlight reaches the earth and so that all living organisms can survive. The heat of the sunlight remains in the atmosphere because Carbon dioxide absorbs the sunlight which is reflected from the earth’s surface. Human lives are completely dependent on the warm sunlight we receive every day. This process of absorbing the warmth of the sunlight instead of leaving them into space is called the Greenhouse effect.

Forest trees are destroyed for many reasons but the primary reason is occupying the space for construction purposes. Vehicle usage among people increased very high in recent years, so burning fossil fuels became uncontrollable. Cut down trees and fossil fuels emission in the atmosphere are the major causes of the pollution in the atmosphere. GHGs automatically increase the temperature of the earth and become hotter than before and finally, it leads to an increase in the greenhouse effect. As a result, the greenhouse effect has risen, as have global average temperatures.

#### 4.2.1.3 Global Warming

Ice sheets and glaciers are melting as a result of global warming. Sea levels increase every year due to melting ice. Rising sea levels would gradually invade low-lying coastal areas. Climate change poses a danger to whole nations, such as the Maldives’ islands. The phenomenon of ocean acidification is also intensified by global warming. Ocean acidification is responsible for many sea creatures’ death. Ocean water absorbs some of the GHGs, due to that ocean water’s temperature becomes high. Warmer, less salty waters may support fewer species.

The ocean water was contaminated heavily and the saltwater was converted into acidic water. This acidic water created a terrible condition for the survival of sea organisms, even the plants in coral. Scientists expect that as the world warms, extreme storms may become more common. Droughts will become more common in some areas, while flooding will become more common in others.

Pollution has already caused severe damage to the environment. The numbers of plants and animals are shrinking due to the effect of contaminated air, water, and land. Pollution spreads rapidly and is now found all over the world, including in the Arctic. Polar bears are starving and dying because their prey seals are no longer available due to contaminated ice sheets.

#### 4.2.1.4 Chlorofluorocarbon

Sun emits harmful radiation known as UV rays or Ultraviolet rays to the earth. UV rays from the sun will increase the risk of skin cancer and have a negative impact on the human immune system. Furthermore, these rays are harmful to almost all organisms, plants, and marine animals. Lives on earth are protected from the sun’s harmful rays by an invisible pale blue protective shield called the ozone layer ([Figure 4.3](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_3)).

![Figure 4.3 Breaking process of oxygen molecules.](images/fig4_3_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.3 Breaking process of oxygen molecules.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_3)

Sun’s UV rays are harmful, but those rays are consumed as energy by Oxygen molecules and transformed into a single atom of Oxygen. Oxygen atoms are bound together to form the Ozone layer. This surface, which is three to five millimeters thick and covers the earth, is three to five millimeters thick.

In the 1960s there was no Chlorofluorocarbon involved in the atmosphere. The ozone started to deplete after the 1970s due to the invention of harmful chemicals. When those chemicals start to emit and mix with other gases in the atmosphere then those emissions affect humans, animals, plants, and finally, it reaches the ozone layer and starts to deplete it. Chlorofluorocarbon is one of the Ozone depletion substances. The ozone layer, an area in Earth’s upper atmosphere, is affected by CFCs. Once the ozone layer is affected, it causes major problems for the environment and humans. When the sun’s UV rays contact the human body directly, they can cause major skin disease, damage the eye retina, and lead to a range of other health problems.

Climate change is also a cause of pollution in the environment. Also affects the ecosystem’s physical and biological components. Furthermore, ozone depletion, GHG emissions, and global warming are mostly causes of environmental pollution. People and governments could reduce air pollution easily and efficiently.

## 4.3 Impact of Marine Transport Pollution

Billions of dollar goods are transported by ship every single day. About 90% of goods are transported through Marine freight globally. Global Marine transportation has positive impacts on economic development but causes negative impacts on the environment. The shipping sector is responsible for >3% of carbon dioxide emissions. The carbon dioxide emissions from shipping are expected to hike 50 to 250% by 2050 – estimation of International Maritime Organization (IMO). This will affect global climate change. Several hazardous gases such as nitrogen oxides (NOx) 15% per year, sulfur oxide (SO[x]) 13% per year, hydrocarbons (HCs), Particulate matter, CO, and carbon dioxide CO[2]) 2.2% per year. Gas emissions data can be tracked by the fuel oil consumption by marine engines. As gas emissions by engines are directly proportional to the total fuel consumption. Operational oil pollution is also one of the factors to be considered.

### 4.3.1 Oil Spills

Among various transportation pollutions caused due to several factors, the oil spill is a major factor that brings a huge impact to the environment. An oil spill in the marine ecosystem during transportation is also a form of pollution caused to the environment. Oil spills not only occur in marine life but also occur in the land in many forms. Example: crude oil and petroleum products from tankers, oil spills offshore platforms. Oil spills also occur during loading and unloading in port. All petroleum products are transported as cargo in ships. In case of any accidental discharge of oil due to any cause of the error, it will result in environmental disaster. Due to the physical and chemical properties of the fuel-efficient aircraft short-haul medium-haul oil, if spilled, it will undergo weathering, dissolution, oxidation, and volatilization, which creates a huge impact on the environment. The cost of cleaning up oil spills is lower than the cost of preventing small oil spills. As the cleaning cost is low, it does not have a huge financial impact.

However, it is a difficult task to clean them up, and it may take weeks, months, or even years. It stays in the same environment for years as sediment. Marine spice’s organs and reproductive cycles are highly damaged due to the contaminants like polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs). Around 30 years ago, people witnessed a major disaster in Alaska. Lakhs of seabirds and thousands of sea otters and a lot more fishes lost their lives due to oil spills. Even though it does not impact humans directly, it indirectly affects those who consume seafood.

### 4.3.2 Ballast Water Pollution

Ships use a huge amount of ballast water to stabilize themselves when they travel without cargo. Cargo ships are stable when filled with cargo, empty cargo ships use ballast water when unloaded with cargo to balance the weight and to stabilize themselves. Marine transportation is a continuously growing sector and predicted to have rapid growth in the next few years. Around 3–5 tons of ballast water is discharged by ships annually. The discharged water is contaminated with oil and other impurities in the ballast tank. This is also called cruise pollution. This affects the marine species, vegetation in the sea, and sea animals as well.

### 4.3.3 Air Pollution

Air pollution is caused by various sources, marine transport is also one of the factors that cause air pollution. Air pollution kills about 4.2 million people, says the World Health Organization (WHO). Most of the ships are powered by combustion engines which emit hazardous gases during their operation. It mixes into the air and causes various health issues to the living organisms. Similar to road vehicles, diesel, and other engines used in ships emit unwanted toxic air through the exhaust called vessels. The major ingredient of the emission is GHG (CO[2]). Despite small passenger ships, cargo, bulk carriers, oil ships contribute the major emission of toxic gases to the environment \[[12](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_12)\].

### 4.3.4 Noise Pollution

Noise pollution is also caused by ships. Ships use loud horns to indicate the presence of their ships to the other ships traveling and also to communicate with the port. Diesel engines and other oil engines also produce noise. Even electric generators used in ships also produce noise. As it is designed in such a manner that the sound will travel for a long distance. This affects the sea species which use sound waves to communicate with each other and the birds that fly to other countries overseas which use sound waves for their navigation also get affected.

### 4.3.5 Sewage Pollution

Large cruise ships can accommodate more than 7,000 passengers in which they travel for a long period. They also dump a million gallons of wastewater into the sea per week. Washing vessels that have dirt, showers, and laundries which contain toxic contents are some of the sources of dirt water. It is named gray water. Cruise ships also discharge harmful chemicals from batteries, chemicals from cleaning the daily utilities, chemicals from expired medicines. This creates a huge threat to the living organisms in the sea. Human wastes from toilets are also deposited into the sea by cruise ships. This form of waste disposal is classified as black water. This affects the marine ecosystem. Bile water which consists of highly concentrated oil that is leaked from the ship engines also pollutes the seawater.

### 4.3.6 Solid Waste Pollution

Solid wastes like plastic covers, steel cans, packing wastes, glasses are thrown off into the sea during travel. They float on the sea surface or deposit in the sea as marine debris. In case of any accidents during the cruise, the ship crashes in the mid-sea, and the materials in the ships are deposited as solid waste inside the sea. Ship breakage is also a form of marine pollution. Sea animals like whales and dolphins get affected by the solid waste deposited in the sea.

### 4.3.7 Physical Damages to Marine

Cruise ships that travel at high speeds cause damage and injuries to the species in the sea. Huge whales strike and get struck in huge ships which causes marine damage. Tins and cans are thrown into the sea deposit above the coral reefs and destroyed. Anchoring of ships and ship accidents also disturbs the growth of the coral reefs.

## 4.4 Environmental Impact of Aviation Industry

The aviation industry is expanding due to increasing passenger demand and also air freight. Big giants in the e-commerce industry offer same-day delivery which comes possible by air freight. Air mode of transportation is the fastest transportation system so far in human invention. Air transport can deliver the goods from one end of the country to the other end within a single day. The same will take a while if using other means of transport (road or ship).

Aviation is the most difficult sector in the transport industry to decarbonize. The aviation industry has taken many actions to make the aviation industry go green. Many kinds of research are going on to use biofuels as aircraft fuel. And also implemented in some of the airlines. Most Airlines are using Biofuel blends which are a mixture of bio and jet fuel and implemented in many commercial aircraft like Virgin Atlantic, United Airlines, and Qantas Airways. Anyways only some of the aircraft use biofuels. It takes time for implementation because of the scale of the industry and also huge investments, government support is needed to implement it in airlines. This would indirectly lead to an increase in ticket fares. There is a 32% increase in CO[2] emissions from commercial aircraft. As the aviation industry is growing faster than expected, the CO[2] emissions would triple in years. Around 5 billion passengers traveled by air in 2019.

The United Kingdom, Germany, The United States, Japan, and China are the top 5 countries that contribute most of the CO[2] emissions in the world. Big players in the aircraft industry have already started manufacturing fuel-efficient planes in which the emission of CO[2] will be reduced. The cost of producing, creating biofuel is high compared to normal jet fuel which creates a break in implementing biofuel. Not only biofuel is a solution there are also many interesting and efficient solutions too. Hydrogen-powered electric planes use hydrogen to power up the electric motor. Hydrogen-powered planes emit only vapor to the environment. And the other emerging solution is electric aircraft that use Lithium-Ion batteries to power up the electric motors in the plane. But it limits the endurance of the plane due to the high weight of the battery compared to fuels.

Future Airplane technologies like supersonic aircraft will burn fuel more than 5–7 times when compared with conventional aircraft, which are super polluting technologies. The aviation industry impacts climate change as well. Other than CO[2] airplanes also emit nitrous gases, water vapor, soot, sulfate, microparticles which lead to roof the factors like climate change, ozone formation, and decrease in methane, sun screening, creation of contrails, and creation of cirrostratus clouds of ice.

There are two major pollutions caused by the aviation industry which are discussed in subsections.

### 4.4.1 Air Pollution

The air enters through the inlet of the aircraft engine and then enters into the combustion chamber combustion of fuel takes place. During the combustion process, it emits some toxic gases like CO[2], Nitrogen oxide, NO[2], and sulfur. They are emitted to the environment through the outlet of the aircraft engine which pollutes the air. During aircraft take-off, the engine works at higher efficiency which pollutes the air in the area around the airport regions. The major problem here is aircraft emit CO[2] and other toxic gases at higher altitudes that will have a greater and direct effect on climate change. Accelerated growth of the aviation industry resulting in the emission of 1.2 billion tons of CO[2] to the environment every year which increases thrice if the same situation continues and no action has been taken.

The major component of aircraft emission is CO[2] which is around 70% of the exhaust. Aircraft emits 3.16 kg of CO[2] for 1 kilogram of fuel consumption. It takes years to remove the emitted GHG from the environment naturally. The Stratospheric ozone layer is destroyed and global warming is caused by the NOx emitted by the aircraft. Aircraft emissions also result in chemical reactions in the atmosphere. Contrails are water vapors from aircraft engines that use fossil fuels and biofuels. At higher altitudes temperature is cold which leads to the freezing of water vapor which produces small ice crystals. The small ice crystals observe water vapor and they form as contrail-included cirrus clouds. It does not stay in the atmosphere for a long time, but contrails are also a factor that needs to be considered because contrails and cirrus clouds block the infra-red rays, which produce a warming effect \[[17](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_17)\], which is more dangerous than the emission of CO[2].

### 4.4.2 Noise Pollution

Aircraft engines have larger blades inside them which rotate at higher RPM, which produces noise. Inside the aircraft’s compression chamber, the air gets compressed and the air is released at high pressure and speed which produces a large amount of noise. It disturbs and causes some serious health issues like sleeping problems, psychological, and cardiac problems to the people who visit near airports. It’s totally under the responsibility of the airport operator. Many measures have been introduced to reduce the noise during flight. Many restrictions on night flying and restrictions in flight paths have been implemented. Every airport should have the mechanisms to monitor sound and follow the restrictions. Noise limits have been given to some of the airports which are present inside the city and these airports will be under serious monitoring. Any operations beyond the limit are identified by the airport authority and the operator will be subjected to serious penalties. Due to increasing air traffic, it is tough to control and monitor the activities which lead to noise pollution.

## 4.5 Artificial Intelligence (AI)

Technology is advancing faster and taking less time to be widely adopted than ever before. Artificial intelligence (AI) is one of the emerging technologies which have created huge impacts in various sectors. ML, computer vision, Algorithms, Big data, and AI are not just words but the future. People think that AI is replacing humans, but it enhances humans. There is a limit where humans cannot go or can be explained as humans cannot do.

AI breaks through all the limits and it has the power to go beyond limits.

AI uses computer vision to sense the environment and ML to create patterns that could work out. AI combined with ML, computer vision can be possibly used for many applications in several sectors, which would result in making the “IMPOSSIBLE” possible. AI is also used to detect and reduce environmental pollution, which is also particularly used in the field of transportation. Over 30% of GHGs emissions are contributed to the environment by the transport sector. This will cause serious effects to humans and the environment, if the same situation continues and if a remedy is not found to reduce the percentage.

AI is growing in various fields even in transportation, where petrol and diesel cars are replaced by electric cars. Cars can now drive themselves in the real-time environment without any help from humans which is possible by using AI, ML, computer vision, neural network, etc., these are now being used to reduce the pollution caused by various forms of transportation. This technology takes the environment into complete surveillance with the help of sensors and IoT and then converts them into models and brings them inside ML which can improve the quality of the forecast report and when it is combined with AI it is possible to accurately predict the environmental situations of a particular environment in advance \[[18](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_18)\]. With those predicted data actions can be taken in advance to avoid risks.

In addition to the collection of sensor data, satellite data, traffic data, and other particular parameters AI and combined physics and chemistry model is used to blend the data from the sources and compare and combine it with the past data. This would result in the prediction of the future environment in advance. Smart machines can be designed to perform tasks with human intelligence. The intelligence of humans is simulated in machines by programming the machines and mimicking human actions.

### 4.5.1 Strength of Artificial Intelligence (AI)

It is now possible to monitor real-time data using AI, which when harnessed gives actionable information to police officers who can anticipate and deter illegal activity. This improves passenger safety. In the same way, monitoring real-time data from autonomous vehicles (AVs) allows for just-in-time interventions to avoid collisions and ensure passenger safety. By proposing alternate routes and estimating wait times at traffic signals, smart roads that collect pedestrian and vehicular data are assisting in reducing traffic congestion and mitigating accidents.

After work, many people find it challenging to drive. In such situations, AVs may provide a rest stop for exhausted drivers, allowing them to nap in the car without endangering themselves or anyone on the road. Similarly, AVs driven by AI can be useful for people who find driving at night challenging. AI also aids in the detection and arrest of erring drivers, such as those who drink and drive or text while driving, making roads safer for all \[[10](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_10)\].

### 4.5.2 Artificial Intelligence (Ai) In Transport Pollution

Using specialized techniques and algorithms, AI is addressing various transportation problems. The air quality index (AQI) is an efficient approach for pollution control. To protect citizens, the government gathers data and places restrictions on cars. Every year, millions of people die as a result of breathing heavily toxic air, while others suffer from serious health problems. ML techniques made a revolution in numerous areas in that air quality was classified and predicted by its classification and regression methods.

## 4.6 Air Quality Index (Aqi)

Knowledge about the quality of the air we breathe is important for every human being. It also helps both people and as well as the government to make various decisions. People can be aware of the current scenario of air quality by the resulting measurement of the AQI. It gives us a report of the quality of air as a routine. Air pollution is hitting the news in metropolitan cities like Delhi, every moment the quality of air increases and decreases \[[1](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_1)\]. When people breathe polluted air, it causes various serious health problems and even it leads to death. That’s when this AQI comes into the picture, people can check the quality of the air they breathe instantly with the help of some mobile applications and websites.

The AQI is measured in the terms of two major resource concentrations of the pollutants for specific intervals of time \[[8](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_8)\]. The government alerts people about the current status of air pollution with the help of the AQI. If the AQI increases, so do the threats to public health. AQIs vary by country according to the emission of pollutants ([Figure 4.4](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_4)).

 

[FIGURE 4.4 Air quality index (AQI) table.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_4)

  
| Air Quality || AQI Value |
| --- | --- |
| Safe || 0–50 |
| --- | --- |
| Average || 51–100 |
| Unsafe to breathe || 101–150 |
| Unsafe for healthy people || 151–200 |
| Highly dangerous || 201+ |

Air quality is the main attribute in the atmosphere. Determining the quality of the air is an expensive process in some techniques, developing countries can’t afford the price of finding the quality of air. AQI is measured by ML techniques that are more cost-effective and resource-efficient \[[10](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_10)\]. The concentration of particulate matter in the air is predicted and classified using ML techniques. Several regression models have different and unique features to predict the quality of air. The predicted value should be accurate to take the appropriate measures.

### 4.6.1 Linear Regression

Interaction between two continuous variables can be reviewed and revised with linear regression. This makes it possible to model the relationship between variables of scalar dependent with explanatory variables by a linear approach \[[7](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_7)\]. A simple linear regression has one descriptive inconsistent value; with more than one value, we have multiple linear regression. Linear regression data set has to be plotted in the graph with the x and y axis. Best fit line or regression line construction based on the least square method. Once the best fit line constructed process of minimizing vertical distance took place to minimize the error ([Figure 4.5](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_5)).

![Figure 4.5 Working process of linear regression.](images/fig4_5_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.5 Working process of linear regression.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_5)

### 4.6.2 Support Vector Regression

Support vector machine (SVM) is a supervised learning method that solves both classification and regression problems. Support Vector Regression carried out with the same feature of SVM. Support vector machine majorly solves classification problems, still helps to identify the robust and reliable values for regression problems \[[6](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_6)\].

Support vector regression constructs a hyperplane with two decision boundaries for the given classes. Hyperplanes will be constructed by analyzing the class and providing the best place to solve the problem, and the positioning is completely dependent on extreme points and vectors. The output of air quality is going to be a continuous numeric variable such problems are solved efficiently using regression analysis. Non-linear separable data fed as input in finding the AQI and that data have to be transformed into high dimensional linear separable data using kernel tricks \[[4](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_4)\]. The goal of Support Vector Regression is to minimize the error in the prediction of the AQI ([Figure 4.6](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_6)).

 ![Figure 4.6 Working process of support vector regression.](images/fig4_6_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.6 Working process of support vector regression.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_6)

### 4.6.3 Random Forest Regression

Random forest algorithm is a bootstrap aggregation of the bagging method. The bagging method creates several base models with a sample data set and the conclusion drawn from those base models. Each base model gets trained on the given sample data. Bootstrap aggregation means data set trained separately in each base model and finally aggregates the result by combining each result of the base model using a voting classifier method.

Random forest regression collects the air quality data set as input data and considers the data to have m rows and n columns. Base learner models are designed to carry out the process of prediction by constructing decision trees. Each decision tree is supplied with a sample data set, sample data sets are drawn in rows and also with replacement \[[19](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_19)\]. Decision trees get trained parallelly with the row sampling with replacement and feature sampling as their input. Individual decision trees predict the air quality as the final output using the fed data set. Decision trees prediction has to be aggregated by analyzing all the decisions of each decision tree ([Figure 4.7](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_7)).

![Figure 4.7 Working process of random forest regression.](images/fig4_7_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.7 Working process of random forest regression.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_7)

### 4.6.4 Adaboost Regressor

AdaBoost algorithm as its name says it boosts the accuracy of ML techniques so it is called a ML ensemble learning (EL) algorithm. Weak students are aided by their weights and require them to vote in the development of a combined final model. AdaBoost Regressor class to solve the regression problem of air quality prediction \[[6](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_6)\]. AdaBoost Regressor begins by fitting the regressor to the dataset and then adjusting the weights based on the error rate.

Boosting assigns weights to classifiers based on their accuracy in predicting a random collection of input cases, and trains the base models one by one. As a result, the more accurate classifiers will contribute more to the final response. Weights are often assigned to each input object based on how difficult it is for all classifiers to predict the instance on average. The higher the weight, the more difficult it is to estimate the instance’s ground truth, and therefore this object has a better chance of being the training subset in the next iteration ([Figure 4.8](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_8)).

![Figure 4.8 Working process of AdaBoost regression.](images/fig4_8_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.8 Working process of AdaBoost regression.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_8)

### 4.6.5 Ridge Regression

Ridge regression is a model tuning technique that can be used to analyze data with multicollinearity. L2 regularization is achieved using this approach. Where there is a problem with multicollinearity, least-squares are unbiased, and variances are high, the expected values are far from the actual values ([Figure 4.9](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_9)).

![Figure 4.9 Working process of ridge regression.](images/fig4_9_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.9 Working process of ridge regression.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_9)

## 4.7 Ai-Based Traffic Monitoring System

Technologies are getting smarter every day. This leads to finding useful solutions to many of the problems in society about 69% of the fuel consumed by the transportation sector but they are not consumed effectively. The vehicles standing in signals idle contribute CO[2] to the environment without any benefits. Around 60 billion gallons of fuel are wasted every year by vehicles idling in traffic \[[5](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_5)\]. There are many kinds of research going on around the world to design a traffic system that reduces idle time at the signs and during peak traffic times. This comes possible by combining and using AI and ML which will result in efficient traffic monitoring and regulating systems. Some of the tech leads use some of the technologies to detect the highly traffic-affected areas and indicate it to the people and also suggest an alternative route. Ex: google maps, AI, ML algorithms, vision sensors, radar, LIDAR, wireless communication, air quality sensors, temperature sensors, and audio sensors are used to analyze the current situation which helps to give priority to emergency vehicles like ambulances, police by directing the signals accordingly.

Vehicle tracking AI systems are tested in some of the countries by the researchers which detect the traffic at 95% accuracy \[[9](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_9)\]. It is possible to determine the speed of the vehicle, classification of the vehicles, and direction of the vehicle with the help of cameras that are placed above the traffic flow. About 400 traffic parameters can be determined by using the neural network at the intersection points \[[15](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_15)\]. The main aims of the smart traffic management system are to analyze real-time traffic circumstances to prioritize moderate traffic conditions, implementing an integrated E-bill payment system to improve conventional ticketing, warn those who violate traffic, to Provide public transit that is both secure and on time, to eliminate unwanted pollution due to traffic, Using GPS and GIS to implement smart traffic control systems at the intersections and road ends to provide accurate traffic guidance and better communication by optimizing road networks.

### 4.7.1 Ai-Based Solutions for Smart Traffic Monitoring and Management

A predetermined signaling scheme is being used in many cities, but it has many disadvantages. It does not consider the current traffic flow and works as defined earlier. But by using the smart traffic control system will analyze the real-time traffic and guide the signals accordingly. Here are some of the smart traffic management systems \[[11](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_11)\]:

*   AI-enabled smart communication and traffic monitoring systems.
*   Traffic signs, smart highways, public transit, smart parking, and a geospatial traffic guidance system are some of the technological elements that make up the smart traffic management system.
*   Applications that automate conventional ticketing systems by offering a user-friendly interface.
*   Vehicle tracking systems like GPS to trace the commutator location.
*   Analyze data from the sensors mounted on the vehicle, road, and highways and inform the vehicle owners in case of any traffic violation.
*   Mobile application that locates the free parking space at a particular location.
*   AI integrated with GPS and sensors to identify less traffic and shortest distance routes to reach a particular destination.
*   LIDAR, AI, ML techniques combined to detect the obstacles around the car which avoids many accidents.
*   3D visualization, RTK, and geographical data can be used to sense the situation of a particular location which allows the user to know the situation more accurately.
*   Traffic queue length can be determined using vision sensors and GIS. And guiding the upcoming vehicle to a different route.

#### 4.7.1.1 Convolutional Neural Network (Cnn)

Many efficient techniques are implemented to improve the quality of travel and also to reduce the environmental pollution caused by various transportation factors. All traffic signals are equipped with cameras, but they are used only for manual monitoring of traffic. But CNN implementation will help in automating the monitoring and detection process. Convolutional neural network (CNN) is a highly accurate algorithm that uses video data to classify the objects which can be used for the classification of road traffic. Compared to other classification algorithms, CNN has a minimized pre-processing. Evaluation is made after training the CNN. The trained model after the evaluation process is now ready to use and it can process the live streaming video and convert it into frames and show the traffic rating ([Figure 4.10](#ch4.xhtml#fig4_10)).

![Figure 4.10 Working process of convolutional neural network.](images/fig4_10_B.jpg)

[Figure 4.10 Working process of convolutional neural network.](#ch4.xhtml#R_fig4_10)

CNN consists of neurons that have learnable weights and biases. A video is a combination of frames or images, and each image is a combination of pixels and each pixel RGB image holds some values which are called tensors that are used as the input for the neural network \[[13](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_13)\]. CNN is made up of multiple layers which are separated by supporting layers. Each layer is made up of neurons, and it is attached to neurons of the previous layer, and the connection goes on to the end of the layer. The classification is made by the end layer and it gives a rating from 0 to 10. The pooling layer is the separation layer that acts as a filter and it modifies the resolution of each frame and output as well. The live video feed is taken from the traffic camera and it is converted into frames and each pixel in the images are analyzed by using CNN and removes the background and separates the vehicles only and after processing it gives the output as a rating ranging from 0–10 which is the classification of traffic level in different lanes by using this technique it is possible to identify the traffic levels in each lane and can guide the signals accordingly to reduce the traffic and also to reduce the pollution caused by the vehicles that are idle in traffic.

#### 4.7.1.2 Artificial Neural Network

Various techniques and methods are used to manage traffic. Among the intelligent methods, Artificial Neural Network is one of the efficient methods. ANN combined with IoT, ML offers a completely reliable solution to the traffic congestion problems. There is also the involvement of some sensors and wireless networks. Traffic density and average speed of vehicles are the main parameters that are considered. Using this data, the Artificial Neural Network classifies the traffic level at each lane.

The entire process is divided into three main segments:

*   Collection of data;
*   Transmission of data; and
*   Processing of data.

In this process, ANN is implemented in the third segment to determine the status of each congestion. ANN helps in processing the data intelligently \[[14](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_14)\]. Artificial neural networks (ANNs) consist of multiple neurons that are connected by weights. The input layer, an output layer, and a hidden layer are the three main layers of the neural network. The traffic data such as vehicle speed and traffic density values are fed as input to the input layer, and no processing is done in this layer. It transfers the values as numbers to the hidden layers which consist of 5 neurons in this condition, and it does all the processing tasks and transfers the output as numbers to the output layer. The output layer consists of the average density of vehicles in a particular lane and the average speed of vehicles in traffic. Before all these the ANNs should be trained with the pre-existing data set. In real-time the traffic data is taken from the sensors and sent to the processing unit by the transmission unit and processing is done using the ANN method and the output is displayed \[[12](#ch4.xhtml#ref4_12)\]. And with the output values, the traffic can be guided accordingly. Queue length can be reduced and idle time of vehicles can be reduced and pollution produced by the vehicles can be controlled.

## 4.8 Conclusion

Pollution spoils our environment in multiple ways. In this chapter, pollution causes and pollutants which are polluting our environment are discussed in detail. Pollution damages agricultural land, air, and water which are essential for living organisms. Air pollution spreads quickly in our environment and is also the reason for multiple health problems in humans, animals, and even also for plants. The WHO released a shocking survey that 9 out of 10 humans breathing polluted air which induced many scientists around the world to work on reducing the impact of air pollution. Inventions like the automated car of Elon Musk to AQI prediction by TensorFlow by college students of Delhi are showing their responsibility to protect our environment. An increase in the number of vehicles is the major reason for transport pollution. Modern techniques are opening their hands to address the problem of transport pollution. AI predicts the quality of air, classifies the pollutants, and provides the best solutions for traffic congestion, AVs, and many more inventions that laid the groundwork for the betterment of the environment. We believe and work for a pollutionfree environment in the future along with the great technology AI.
